Implementation:
```
def smooth_loss(z, eps):
  p = softmax(z)
  c = conv(p,p)
  cm = c.max()
  s = (c - cm)/eps
  w = np.exp(s - s.max()); w/=w.sum()
  L = cm + eps * np.log(w.sum())
  # grad_p = 2*(w @ A)  # A depends p
  A = _build_A(p)
  grad_p = 2.0 * (w @ A)  # length n
  grad_z = p * (grad_p - np.dot(p, grad_p))
  return L, grad_z
```

9. `_optimize(a0, stop, seed, lp_iter_limit)`:
Pseudo:
```
def _optimize(a0, stop, seed=0, use_mint=True, max_iters=100000):
  n=a0.size
  a=_normalize(a0)
  best_a=a.copy(); best_M=_M(a); best_score=_score_internal(a)
  rng=np.random.default_rng(seed)
  prev=None; stale=0; pert_sigma=0.01; it=0
  # maybe cache A? rebuilt each iter
  while time.monotonic()<stop-0.2:
    it+=1
    c=_conv(a,a); M=float(c.max())
    if M < best_M: ... update local and maybe global? We'll update global outside? We can pass callback.
    if it>1 and abs(M-prev)<1e-13: 
       ...
    # LP maxsum
    improved=False
    if (it % 1 ==0):
       A=_build_A(a)
       g=_lp_maxsum(A,M,n)
       if g is not None:
           if not np.allclose(g,a,atol=1e-6):
              cand,Mcand=_line_search_mix(a,g,M, samples=64)
              if cand is not None and Mcand < M - 1e-12:
                  a=cand; improved=True; stale=0; continue
              # maybe use direct g not blend?

def _convolve(a,b=None):
    if b is None: b=a
    n=a.size
    if a.size < 350 or b.size < 350:
        return np.convolve(a,b)
    L=scipy_fft.next_fast_len(2*n-1)
    return np.fft.irfft(np.fft.rfft(a,L)*np.fft.rfft(b,L), L)[:2*n-1]
```

def _gen_inits(n, base):
  inits=[base]
  x=np.linspace(0,1,n)
  # Beta densities
  for a,beta in [(0.5,0.5),(0.3,0.3),(1,2),(2,1),(0.7,0.7),(1.5,1.5),(0.25,2),(2,0.25)]:
      y=x**a * (1-x)**beta with endpoints handling; y=max(y,1e-6); inits.append(y/sum)
  # cosine harmonics
  rng=...
  for _ in range(9):
      h=np.ones(n)
      for m in [1,2,3]:
          amp = rng.uniform(0.05,0.35) if rng else ...
          h += amp*np.cos(2*np.pi*m*x + rng.uniform(0,2π))
      h=np.maximum(h,1e-6); inits.append(h/sum)
  # blend base with random
  for _ in range(5): inits.append( (0.8*(base/sum)+0.2*randomclip)/sum )
```

Implement `_smooth_step(a, stop, max_iter=200, eps=None)`:
```
def _smooth_step(a, stop, tau=None, max_iter=120):
  n=a.size
  M=_M(a)
  eps = M/30.0 if tau is None
  z=np.log(a+1e-12)
  def fun(z):
     p=softmax(z); c=_conv(p,p); cm=c.max(); s=(c-cm)/eps; s-=s.max(); w=np.exp(s); ws=w.sum(); L=cm+eps*np.log(ws); A=_build_A(p); grad_p=2*(w/ws)@A; grad_z=p*(grad_p - p.dot(grad_p)); return L, grad_z
  res=minimize(fun,z,jac=True,method='L-BFGS-B',options={'maxiter':max_iter,'ftol':1e-15,'gtol':1e-8})
  p=softmax(res.x); Mnew=_M(p); return p, Mnew
```

```
def _active_gradient(a, c, M, tol=1e-5):
   active = np.where(c >= M*(1-tol))[0]
   n=len(a)
   grad=np.zeros(n)
   for k in active:
      js = np.arange(max(0,k-n+1), min(n-1,k)+1)
      idx = k - js
      grad[js] += 2*a[idx]
   grad /= max(1,len(active))
   grad -= grad.mean()
   return grad
```
This loops active count maybe up to 400, each up to 201 ->80k; okay. Use only fallback.

Now, let's design time-bounded `_optimize` robustly:

```
def _optimize(a0, stop, seed=0, lp_every=1, mint_every=5):
    a = _normalize(a0)
    n = a.size
    best_a = a.copy(); best_M = _M(a)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)
    A = None
    stale = 0
    sigma = 0.01
    it = 0
    prev_M = None
    # For random perturb count
    while time.monotonic() < stop - 0.05:
        c = _conv(a,a); M = float(c.max())
        if M < best_M - 1e-14:
            best_M = M; best_a = a.copy(); stale=0
        it += 1
        improved = False
        # LP
        if it % lp_every == 0:
            A = _build_A(a)
            g = _lp_maxsum(A, M, n, time_limit)
            if g is not None:
                directions = [g]
                # if symmetric diff
                if not np.allclose(g, g[::-1], atol=1e-4):
                    directions.append((g+g[::-1])/2)
                for d in directions:
                    if not np.allclose(d, a, atol=1e-7):
                       cand, Mc = _line_search_mix(a,d,M, 48)
                       if cand is not None and Mc < M - 1e-12:
                           a = cand; improved=True; break
                if improved: 
                    stale=0; continue
        # min-t if stale or every mint
        if not improved and (stale>2 or it % mint_every ==0):
            if A is None: A=_build_A(a)
            mg, tmin = _lp_mint(A,M,n,time_limit)
            if mg is not None and not np.allclose(mg,a,atol=1e-7):
                cand, Mc = _line_search_mix(a,mg,M,48)
                if cand is not None and Mc < M - 1e-12:
                    a=cand; improved=True; stale=0; continue
        # gradient
        if not improved:
            grad = _subgrad_direction(a,c,M)
            if grad is not None:
                d = -grad; gnorm=np.linalg.norm(d)
                if gnorm>1e-12:
                    d/=gnorm
                    loga=np.log(a+1e-15)
                    for eta in (0.01,0.02,0.05,0.1):
                        cand=np.exp(loga + eta*d)
                        # possible overflow? d norm1, eta small, okay
                        cand=np.maximum(cand,0); s=cand.sum()
                        if s>0:
                           cand/=s; Mc=_M(cand)
                           if Mc < M - 1e-12:
                               a=cand; improved=True; break
                    if improved:
                        stale=0; continue
        # no improvement: perturb
        a = a * np.exp(rng.normal(0,sigma,n))
        a = np.maximum(a, 1e-12)
        s=a.sum(); if s>0: a/=s
        stale +=1
        if stale % 10 ==0:
            sigma = min(0.5, sigma*1.4)
        if stale > 60:
            # restart from best or random blend
            if rng.random()<0.5:
                a = best_a.copy()
            else:
                r = np.ones(n)*rng.random(n) # maybe uniform plus random
                a = 0.5*best_a + 0.5*r
            a=_normalize(a); sigma=0.01; stale=10
        # if remaining small break
    # final: maybe line search sym direction?
    d=_normalize(0.5*(best_a+best_a[::-1]))
    cand,Mc=_line_search_mix(best_a,d,best_M,48)
    if cand... update
    return best_a, best_M
```

Use fixed 0.25 for n<=300 else 0.5. But if HiGHS presolve fast, fine. If one LP exceeds, it aborts. We don't want one LP to eat too much. Set `lp_limit = min(0.5, max(0.1, 0.05*(stop-now)))`? Now, smooth optimization after LP:
```
def _smooth_polish(a0, stop, n):
    a=_normalize(a0); M=_M(a); best_a=a.copy(); best_M=M
    if minimize is None: return best_a
    eps_scale = [1/20,1/40,1/80,1/160,1/320]
    for factor in eps_scale:
       if time>stop-5: break
       eps = max(1e-8, M*factor)
       z0=np.log(a+1e-12)
       maxiter = if n<400: 150 else 80
       def fun(z): ...
       try:
          res=minimize(fun,z0,jac=True,method='L-BFGS-B',options={'maxiter':maxiter,'ftol':1e-15,'gtol':1e-7})

idx_cache={}
def _get_mask(n):
   if n not in cache:
      rows=np.arange(2*n-1)[:,None]
      cols=np.arange(n)[None,:]
      diff=rows-cols
      mask=(diff>=0)&(diff<n)
      idx=diff.clip(0,n-1)
      cache[n]=(mask.astype(bool), idx.astype(np.intp))
   return cache[n]

def _build_A(a):
   n=a.size; mask,idx=...; return np.where(mask, a[idx], 0.0)
```

def _conv(a,b=None):

`_line_search_mix`:
```
def _line_search_mix(a,b,Mcur, coarse=48, fine=64):
    if b is None or not finite: return None, Mcur
    b=_norm(b)
    if np.linalg.norm(a-b) < 1e-12: return None, Mcur
    aa=_conv(a,a); ab=_conv(a,b); bb=_conv(b,b)
    def eval_lams(lams):
       t1=1-lams; t2=lams
       vals = aa[None,:]*(t1*t1)[:,None] + 2*ab[None,:]*(t1*t2)[:,None] + bb[None,:]*(t2*t2)[:,None]
       return vals.max(axis=1)
    lams=np.linspace(0,1,coarse)
    Mv=eval_lams(lams)
    if not finite: return None
    i=int(np.argmin(Mv)); best_val=float(Mv[i]); best_l=float(lams[i]); step=1.0/(coarse-1) if coarse>1 else 0.1
    lo=max(0.0,best_l-2*step); hi=min(1.0,best_l+2*step)
    if hi-lo>1e-12:
       lams2=np.linspace(lo,hi,fine)
       Mv2=eval_lams(lams2)
       j=np.argmin(Mv2); v=float(Mv2[j]); l=float(lams2[j]); if v<best_val: ...
    if best_val < Mcur - 1e-13:
       cand=(1-best_l)*a+best_l*b
       cand=_norm(cand)
       Mc=_M(cand)
       return cand, Mc
    return None, best_val
```
Need if eval_lams uses huge memory? okay. `vals` shape coarse*m. If n=500,m=999, 48k. Good.

Use `Mcur - 1e-13`; if Mcur~0.003, absolute 1e-13 very small. Might accept zero. Good.

`_lp_maxsum`:
```
def _lp_maxsum(A, M, n, stop, lp_limit):
   c_obj=-np.ones(n)
   b_ub=np.full(A.shape[0], M*(1+1e-8))
   try:
      res=linprog(c_obj, A_ub=A, b_ub=b_ub, bounds=[(0.0,None)]*n, method='highs',
                  options={'time_limit': lp_limit, 'dual_feasibility_tolerance':1e-9, 'primal_feasibility_tolerance':1e-9, 'presolve':True})
   except Exception: return None
   if res.status !=0 or res.x is None: return None
   g=np.asarray(res.x,float); gsum=float(g.sum())
   if gsum <= 0 or not finite: return None
   return g/gsum
```
If gsum much less than1? Since feasible, but due to solver maybe. But return normalized.

`_lp_mint`:
```
def _lp_mint(A, M, n, lp_limit):
   m=A.shape[0]
   Aaug=np.empty((m,n+1),float)
   Aaug[:,:n]=A
   Aaug[:,n]=0
   # set diagonal -1: Aaug[np.arange(m), n] = -1.0
   c_obj=np.zeros(n+1); c_obj[-1]=1.0
   Aeq=np.zeros((1,n+1)); Aeq[0,:n]=1.0
   bounds=[(0.0,None)]*n + [(0.0,M)]
   try:
     res=linprog(c_obj, A_ub=Aaug, b_ub=np.zeros(m), A_eq=Aeq, b_eq=np.array([1.0]), bounds=bounds, method='highs', options=...)
   except: return None, None
   if res.status !=0 or res.x is None: return None,None
   g=res.x[:n]; t=float(res.x[-1])
   gsum=float(g.sum())
   if gsum<=0 or not finite: return None,None
   return g/gsum, t
```
Need Aaug construction can be optimized by `np.hstack((A, -np.eye(m)))`. `np.empty` and set diag. Since A is 2D, `Aaug[:, n] = -1` works for one column? Yes. The diagonal positions (m,n) but column n scalar; `Aaug[np.arange(m), n] = -1.0`.

`_active_grad`:
```
def _active_grad(a,c,M,tol=1e-5):
   active=np.where(c >= M*(1-tol))[0]
   if active.size==0: return None
   n=a.size; grad=np.zeros(n)
   for k in active:
       js=np.arange(max(0,k-n+1), min(n-1,k)+1)
       grad[js] += 2.0 * a[k-js]
   grad /= active.size
   grad -= grad.mean()
   norm=np.linalg.norm(grad)
   return grad if norm>1e-12 else None
```
If active includes many, loop. Could use precomputed rows: if A built and available, `grad=2*A[active].mean(axis=0)`. In `_optimize`, A may be built from LP. We can use that to speed. Let's implement gradient using A if not None:
```
if A is not None:
   act = np.where(c >= M*(1-1e-5))[0]
   grad = 2.0 * A[act].mean(axis=0) - scalar mean
```

def _inits_from_base(base, n, rng, count=12):
    inits=[_norm(base)]
    # noisy variants
    for sig in [0.02,0.05,0.1,0.15]:
       y=base*np.exp(rng.normal(0,sig,n)); inits.append(_norm(y))
    # sym
    y=0.5*(base+base[::-1]); inits.append(_norm(y))
    x=np.linspace(0,1,n)
    # if n>1 else...
    for alp,bet in [...]:
       y=(x+1e-6)**alp * ((1-x)+1e-6)**bet
       inits.append(_norm(y))
    # sum-edge power
    for pwr in [0.5,1,2]:
       y=x**pwr+(1-x)**pwr+1e-8
       y+=0.2 # add center? maybe
       inits.append(_norm(y))
    # random harmonic
    for _ in range(6):
       y=np.ones(n)
       for m in [1,2,3,4]:
          if rng.random()<0.7:
             amp=rng.uniform(0.05,0.4)/max(1,m*0.5)
             y += amp*np.cos(2*np.pi*m*x + rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi))
       y=np.maximum(y,1e-6); inits.append(_norm(y))
    # blend base with random
    for _ in range(3):
       r=np.linspace(0,1,n)? or rng.random(n)
       y=0.7*base +0.3*(rng.random(n)+1e-6)
       inits.append(_norm(y))
    return inits
```

Direct active grad:
```
def _active_grad(a,c,M):
   active=np.where(c >= M*(1-1e-5))[0]
   n=a.size; grad=np.zeros(n)
   for k in active:
      js = np.arange(max(0,k-n+1), min(n-1,k)+1)
      grad[js] += 2.0 * a[k-js]
   grad /= active.size
   grad -= grad.mean()
   return grad
```
Use if A is None.

Top-level `_norm`:
```
def _norm(a):
   a=np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
   if a.size==0: a=np.ones(32)
   a=np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
   a=np.clip(a,0.0,1000.0)
   s=a.sum()
   if s<=1e-15:
      return np.ones(a.size, dtype=np.float64)/a.size
   return a/s
```
If a contains nan, replaced 0. Good.

Top-level `_get_parent`:
```
def _get_parent():
   arr=None
   try: arr=np.asarray(globals()['height_sequence_1'], dtype=np.float64).ravel()
   except: pass
   if arr is None or arr.size==0:
       arr=np.ones(201)
   return _norm(arr)
```
If all zeros, norm returns uniform length. Good.

def _fallback_next_fast_len(n):
    if n<=0: return 1
    import math
    return 1 << (int(math.ceil(math.log2(n))))

Precompute:
```
rows=np.arange(2*n-1)[:,None]
cols=np.arange(n)[None,:]
diff=rows-cols
mask=(diff>=0)&(diff<n)
idx=np.clip(diff,0,n-1).astype(np.intp)
cache[n]=(mask,idx)
```

Now, `_active_direct`:
```
def _active_direct(a,c,M):
   active = np.where(c >= M*(1.0-1e-5))[0]
   if active.size ==0: return None
   grad=np.zeros(a.size)
   for k in active:
      js = np.arange(max(0, int(k)-a.size+1), min(a.size-1, int(k))+1)
      grad[js] += 2.0 * a[int(k)-js]
   grad /= active.size
   grad -= grad.mean()
   if np.linalg.norm(grad) <= 1e-12: return None
   return grad
```

In `_optimize`, using A:
```
act = np.where(c >= M*(1-1e-5))[0]
grad = 2.0 * A[act].mean(axis=0)
grad -= grad.mean()
```
If A None, call direct.

def _heuristic_batch(n, rng, count=30):
    out=[]
    if n<=1: return [np.ones(n)]
    x=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n)
    eps=1e-9
    # cosine modes
    for mode in [0,1,2,3,4]:
       if mode==0: continue
       for q in [0.3,0.6,0.9]:
          y=1.0 - q*np.cos(np.pi*x)  # high? try both signs
          out.append(_norm(np.maximum(y,eps)))
          if mode>1:
          y=1.0 + q*np.cos(2*np.pi*mode*x)
          out.append(_norm(np.maximum(y,eps)))
    # sin center trough
    for q in [0.3,0.6,0.9]:
       y=1.0 - q*np.sin(np.pi*x) # low center? sin 0 edges
       out.append(...)
       y=1.0 + q*np.sin(np.pi*x)
    # beta
    for alp,bet in [(0.5,0.5),(0.3,0.3),(0.5,1.0),(1.0,0.5),(0.3,1.5),(1.5,0.3),(0.8,0.8)]:
       y=np.power(x+eps,alp)*np.power(1-x+eps,bet)
       out.append(_norm(np.maximum(y,eps)))
    # edge mixtures
    for p in [0.3,0.5,1,2]:
       y=np.power(x+eps,p)+np.power(1-x+eps,p)+eps
       out.append(_norm(y))
    # random harmonics
    for _ in range(10):
       y=np.ones(n)
       for m in [1,2,3,4,5]:
          if rng.random()<0.7:
             amp=rng.uniform(0.05,0.5)/(m+1)
             y += amp*np.cos(2*np.pi*m*x + rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi))
             y2 = y? no
       y=np.maximum(y,eps)
       out.append(_norm(y))
    return out[:count]
```
Some modes q high may produce negative if q>1? q=0.9 fine. `1 - q*cos(π x)` endpoints 1±0.9? if q=0.9 min0.1. Good. `1+q*cos` min0.1. We'll clip.

def _safe_eval(a):
   if eval_func is None: return float('inf')
   a=np.asarray(a,dtype=float).ravel()
   a=np.clip(a,0,1000)
   if a.size==0 or not np.isfinite(a).any(): return inf
   try: return float(eval_func([float(x) for x in a]))
   except Exception: return inf

def _active_direction(a,c,M,A):
   act = np.where(c >= M*(1-1e-5))[0]
   if act.size==0: return None
   if A is not None:
      grad = 2.0 * A[act].mean(axis=0)
   else:
      grad = _active_grad_direct(a,c,M)
      if grad is None: return None
   grad -= grad.mean()
   if norm <=1e-12 return None
   return grad
```

def _softmax(z):
    z=np.asarray(z,float); if z.size==0: return array([1]); z=z-z.max(); e=np.exp(z); s=e.sum(); if s<=0: return ones/z.size; return e/s
```

```
def _softmax(z):
   z=np.asarray(z,float).ravel()
   if z.size==0: return np.array([1.0])
   if not np.all(np.isfinite(z)): return np.ones(z.size)/z.size
   z=z-z.max()
   e=np.exp(z)
   s=e.sum()
   if s<=0 or not finite: return ones
   return e/s
```
Good.

def _conv(a,b=None):
   if b is None: b=a
   na=a.size; nb=b.size; out=na+nb-1
   if max(na,nb) < 350: return np.convolve(a,b)
   L=_next_fast_len(out)
   fa=np.fft.rfft(a,L); fb=np.fft.rfft(b,L)
   c=np.fft.irfft(fa*fb,L)[:out]
   c=np.asarray(c,float)
   c[c<0]=0.0
   return c
```

```python
import numpy as np
import time
import math
from scipy.optimize import linprog

try:
    from scipy.optimize import minimize
except Exception:
    minimize = None

try:
    from scipy.fft import next_fast_len as _next_fast_len
except Exception:
    def _next_fast_len(n):
        if n <= 1:
            return 1
        return 1 << int(math.ceil(math.log2(n)))


def _norm_arr(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if a.size == 0:
        a = np.ones(32, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 1e-15:
        return np.ones(a.size, dtype=np.float64) / float(a.size)
    return a / s


def _softmax_arr(z):
    z = np.asarray(z, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    if z.size == 0:
        return np.array([1.0], dtype=np.float64)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(z)):
        return np.ones(z.size, dtype=np.float64) / float(z.size)
    z = z - float(z.max())
    e = np.exp(z)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        return np.ones(z.size, dtype=np.float64) / float(z.size)
    return e / s


def _get_parent():
    try:
        arr = np.asarray(globals()["height_sequence_1"], dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    except Exception:
        arr = None
    if arr is None or arr.size == 0:
        arr = np.ones(201, dtype=np.float64) / 201.0
    return _norm_arr(arr)


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1300, **kwargs):
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    budget = float(budget_s)
    safety = min(12.0, max(0.5, 0.02 * budget))
    deadline = t0 + budget - safety
    if deadline <= t0:
        deadline = t0 + max(0.1, budget)

    eval_func = globals().get("evaluate_sequence", None)
    rng_global = np.random.default_rng(int(seed))

    parent = _get_parent()
    n_parent = int(parent.size)

    idx_cache = {}

    def _get_indices(n):
        n = int(n)
        if n not in idx_cache:
            rows = np.arange(2 * n - 1, dtype=np.int64)[:, None]
            cols = np.arange(n, dtype=np.int64)[None, :]
            diff = rows - cols
            mask = (diff >= 0) & (diff < n)
            idx = np.clip(diff, 0, n - 1).astype(np.int64)
            idx_cache[n] = (mask, idx)
        return idx_cache[n]

    def _build_A(a):
        n = int(a.size)
        mask, idx = _get_indices(n)
        return np.where(mask, a[idx], 0.0)

    def _conv(a, b=None):
        if b is None:
            b = a
        a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        b = np.asarray(b, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        out = a.size + b.size - 1
        if max(a.size, b.size) < 350:
            return np.convolve(a, b)
        L = _next_fast_len(out)
        fa = np.fft.rfft(a, L)
        fb = np.fft.rfft(b, L)
        c = np.fft.irfft(fa * fb, L)[:out]
        c = np.asarray(c, dtype=np.float64)
        c[c < 0.0] = 0.0
        return c

    def _M(a):
        a = _norm_arr(a)
        c = _conv(a, a)
        return float(max(0.0, float(c.max())))

    def _internal_score(a):
        a = _norm_arr(a)
        return 2.0 * float(a.size) * _M(a)

    def _safe_eval(a):
        if eval_func is None:
            return float("inf")
        a = _norm_arr(a)
        if a.size == 0:
            return float("inf")
        try:
            return float(eval_func([float(x) for x in a]))
        except Exception:
            return float("inf")

    best_seq = _norm_arr(parent).copy()
    initial_int = _internal_score(best_seq)
    initial_exact = _safe_eval(best_seq)
    best_score = initial_exact if np.isfinite(initial_exact) else initial_int
    if not np.isfinite(best_score):
        best_score = 2.0

    def _consider(a, force=False):
        nonlocal best_seq, best_score
        a = _norm_arr(a)
        if a.size == 0:
            return
        sc_int = _internal_score(a)
        if force or sc_int < best_score + 1e-7:
            sc = _safe_eval(a)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
                best_score = float(sc)
                best_seq = a.copy()
            elif (not np.isfinite(sc)) and sc_int < best_score:
                best_score = float(sc_int)
                best_seq = a.copy()

    def _line_search_mix(a, b, Mcur, coarse=48, fine=64):
        a = _norm_arr(a)
        b = _norm_arr(b)
        if a.size != b.size:
            return None, float(Mcur)
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(a)) or not np.all(np.isfinite(b)):
            return None, float(Mcur)
        if float(np.linalg.norm(a - b)) < 1e-13:
            return None, float(Mcur)

        aa = _conv(a, a)
        ab = _conv(a, b)
        bb = _conv(b, b)

        def eval_lams(lams):
            lams = np.asarray(lams, dtype=np.float64)
            t1 = 1.0 - lams
            t2 = lams
            vals = (
                aa[None, :] * (t1 * t1)[:, None]
                + 2.0 * ab[None, :] * (t1 * t2)[:, None]
                + bb[None, :] * (t2 * t2)[:, None]
            )
            return vals.max(axis=1)

        lams = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, int(coarse))
        Mv = eval_lams(lams)
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(Mv)):
            return None, float(Mcur)

        i = int(np.argmin(Mv))
        best_val = float(Mv[i])
        best_l = float(lams[i])
        step = 1.0 / float(max(1, coarse - 1))

        lo = max(0.0, best_l - 2.0 * step)
        hi = min(1.0, best_l + 2.0 * step)
        if hi - lo > 1e-12:
            lams2 = np.linspace(lo, hi, int(fine))
            Mv2 = eval_lams(lams2)
            if np.all(np.isfinite(Mv2)):
                j = int(np.argmin(Mv2))
                v2 = float(Mv2[j])
                l2 = float(lams2[j])
                if v2 < best_val:
                    best_val = v2
                    best_l = l2

        if best_val < float(Mcur) - 1e-13:
            cand = (1.0 - best_l) * a + best_l * b
            cand = _norm_arr(cand)
            Mc = _M(cand)
            if Mc < float(Mcur) - 1e-14:
                return cand, Mc
        return None, best_val

    def _lp_maxsum(A, M, n, lp_limit):
        n = int(n)
        if n <= 0:
            return None
        c_obj = -np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
        b_ub = np.full(A.shape[0], float(M) * (1.0 + 1e-8), dtype=np.float64)
        try:
            res = linprog(
                c_obj,
                A_ub=A,
                b_ub=b_ub,
                bounds=[(0.0, None)] * n,
                method="highs",
                options={"time_limit": float(lp_limit)},
            )
        except Exception:
            return None
        if res is None or getattr(res, "status", -1) != 0 or res.x is None:
            return None
        g = np.asarray(res.x, dtype=np.float64)
        g = np.where(np.isfinite(g), g, 0.0)
        g = np.maximum(g, 0.0)
        gs = float(g.sum())
        if gs <= 1e-15:
            return None
        return g / gs

    def _lp_mint(A, M, n, lp_limit):
        n = int(n)
        m = int(A.shape[0])
        A_aug = np.empty((m, n + 1), dtype=np.float64)
        A_aug[:, :n] = A
        A_aug[:, n] = 0.0
        A_aug[np.arange(m), n] = -1.0

        c_obj = np.zeros(n + 1, dtype=np.float64)
        c_obj[-1] = 1.0

        A_eq = np.zeros((1, n + 1), dtype=np.float64)
        A_eq[0, :n] = 1.0
        b_eq = np.array([1.0], dtype=np.float64)

        bounds = [(0.0, None)] * n + [(0.0, float(M))]
        try:
            res = linprog(
                c_obj,
                A_ub=A_aug,
                b_ub=np.zeros(m, dtype=np.float64),
                A_eq=A_eq,
                b_eq=b_eq,
                bounds=bounds,
                method="highs",
                options={"time_limit": float(lp_limit)},
            )
        except Exception:
            return None, None
        if res is None or getattr(res, "status", -1) != 0 or res.x is None:
            return None, None
        g = np.asarray(res.x[:n], dtype=np.float64)
        t = float(res.x[-1])
        g = np.where(np.isfinite(g), g, 0.0)
        g = np.maximum(g, 0.0)
        gs = float(g.sum())
        if gs <= 1e-15 or not np.isfinite(t):
            return None, None
        return g / gs, t

    def _active_grad_direct(a, c, M):
        n = int(a.size)
        act = np.where(c >= M * (1.0 - 1e-5))[0]
        if act.size == 0:
            return None
        grad = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        for kk in act:
            k = int(kk)
            lo = max(0, k - n + 1)
            hi = min(n - 1, k)
            js = np.arange(lo, hi + 1, dtype=np.int64)
            grad[js] += 2.0 * a[k - js]
        grad /= float(act.size)
        grad -= float(grad.mean())
        if float(np.linalg.norm(grad)) <= 1e-12:
            return None
        return grad

    def _active_direction(a, c, M, A):
        act = np.where(c >= M * (1.0 - 1e-5))[0]
        if act.size == 0:
            return None
        if A is not None:
            grad = 2.0 * A[act].mean(axis=0)
            grad = np.asarray(grad, dtype=np.float64)
        else:
            grad = _active_grad_direct(a, c, M)
            if grad is None:
                return None
        grad -= float(grad.mean())
        if float(np.linalg.norm(grad)) <= 1e-12:
            return None
        return grad

    def _optimize(a0, stop, seed=0, lp_every=1, mint_every=8):
        a = _norm_arr(a0)
        n = int(a.size)
        if n <= 1:
            return a.copy(), _M(a)

        best_a = a.copy()
        best_M = _M(a)
        rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed))
        A = None
        stale = 0
        sigma = 0.01
        it = 0
        lp_fail = 0

        while True:
            now = time.monotonic()
            rem = stop - now
            if rem < 0.25:
                break

            c = _conv(a, a)
            M = float(c.max())
            if M < best_M - 1e-14:
                best_M = M
                best_a = a.copy()

            it += 1
            improved = False

            rem = stop - time.monotonic()
            if rem < 0.25:
                break
            cap = 0.6 if n > 350 else 0.3
            lp_limit = min(cap, max(0.05, 0.02 * rem))

            if it % max(1, lp_every) == 0:
                A = _build_A(a)
                g = _lp_maxsum(A, M, n, lp_limit)
                if g is None:
                    lp_fail += 1
                    if lp_fail > 8:
                        lp_every = max(2, lp_every)
                else:
                    lp_fail = 0
                    dirs = [(g + 1e-12) / (g.sum() + 1e-30)]
                    if not np.allclose(g, g[::-1], atol=1e-4):
                        symg = 0.5 * (g + g[::-1])
                        sg = float(symg.sum())
                        if sg > 0:
                            dirs.append(symg / sg)

                    for d in dirs:
                        d = np.asarray(d, dtype=np.float64)
                        ds = float(d.sum())
                        if ds <= 0 or not np.all(np.isfinite(d)):
                            continue
                        d = np.maximum(d, 0.0)
                        d = d / float(d.sum())
                        if np.allclose(d, a, atol=1e-8):
                            continue
                        cand, Mc = _line_search_mix(a, d, M, coarse=48, fine=64)
                        if cand is not None and Mc < M - 1e-12:
                            a = cand
                            improved = True
                            stale = 0
                            break

                if improved:
                    continue

            if not improved and (stale > 2 or it % max(1, mint_every) == 0):
                if A is None:
                    A = _build_A(a)
                rem = stop - time.monotonic()
                if rem > 0.3:
                    cap = 0.8 if n > 350 else 0.35
                    lp_limit = min(cap, max(0.05, 0.02 * rem))
                    mg, tmin = _lp_mint(A, M, n, lp_limit)
                    if mg is not None and not np.allclose(mg, a, atol=1e-8):
                        cand, Mc = _line_search_mix(a, mg, M, coarse=48, fine=64)
                        if cand is not None and Mc < M - 1e-12:
                            a = cand
                            improved = True
                            stale = 0
                            continue

            if not improved:
                d = _active_direction(a, c, M, A)
                if d is not None:
                    d = -d
                    dn = float(np.linalg.norm(d))
                    if dn > 1e-12:
                        d = d / dn
                        loga = np.log(a + 1e-15)
                        for eta in (0.008, 0.016, 0.032, 0.064):
                            cand = np.exp(loga + eta * d)
                            cand = np.where(np.isfinite(cand), cand, 0.0)
                            cand = np.maximum(cand, 0.0)
                            s = float(cand.sum())
                            if s <= 1e-15:
                                continue
                            cand = cand / s
                            Mc = _M(cand)
                            if Mc < M - 1e-12:
                                a = cand
                                improved = True
                                stale = 0
                                break
                    if improved:
                        continue

            if not improved and stale % 3 == 0:
                sym = 0.5 * (a + a[::-1])
                sym = _norm_arr(sym)
                if not np.allclose(sym, a, atol=1e-10):
                    cand, Mc = _line_search_mix(a, sym, M, coarse=32, fine=48)
                    if cand is not None and Mc < M - 1e-12:
                        a = cand
                        improved = True
                        stale = 0
                        continue

            if not improved:
                a = a * np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, sigma, n))
                a = np.maximum(a, 1e-14)
                s = float(a.sum())
                if s <= 0:
                    a = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / n
                else:
                    a = a / s
                stale += 1
                if stale % 10 == 0:
                    sigma = min(0.45, sigma * 1.45)
                if stale > 70:
                    if rng.random() < 0.55:
                        a = best_a.copy()
                    else:
                        r = np.maximum(rng.random(n), 1e-6)
                        a = 0.55 * best_a + 0.45 * r
                    a = _norm_arr(a)
                    sigma = 0.01
                    stale = 10

        # One last cheap symmetry / gradient pass on the local best.
        a = best_a
        M = _M(a)
        c = _conv(a, a)
        sym = _norm_arr(0.5 * (a + a[::-1]))
        if not np.allclose(sym, a, atol=1e-10):
            cand, Mc = _line_search_mix(a, sym, M, coarse=32, fine=48)
            if cand is not None and Mc < best_M - 1e-12:
                best_a = cand
                best_M = Mc
        return best_a, best_M

    def _heuristic_batch(n, rng, count=30):
        n = int(n)
        if n <= 1:
            return [np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)]
        out = []
        x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n, dtype=np.float64)
        eps = 1e-9

        for q in (0.25, 0.5, 0.75, 0.95):
            y = 1.0 - q * np.cos(np.pi * x)
            out.append(_norm_arr(np.maximum(y, eps)))
            y = 1.0 + q * np.cos(np.pi * x)
            out.append(_norm_arr(np.maximum(y, eps)))
            if n > 5:
                y = 1.0 - q * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * x)
                out.append(_norm_arr(np.maximum(y, eps)))
                y = 1.0 + q * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * x)
                out.append(_norm_arr(np.maximum(y, eps)))
                y = 1.0 - 0.8 * np.cos(4.0 * np.pi * x)
                out.append(_norm_arr(np.maximum(y, eps)))

        for al, be in (
            (0.5, 0.5),
            (0.3, 0.3),
            (0.5, 1.0),
            (1.0, 0.5),
            (0.3, 1.5),
            (1.5, 0.3),
            (0.8, 0.8),
            (1.2, 1.2),
        ):
            y = (x + eps) ** al * (1.0 - x + eps) ** be
            out.append(_norm_arr(np.maximum(y, eps)))

        for p in (0.25, 0.5, 0.8, 1.0, 1.6, 2.5):
            y = (x + eps) ** p + (1.0 - x + eps) ** p + eps
            out.append(_norm_arr(y))

        for _ in range(10):
            y = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
            for m in (1, 2, 3, 4, 5):
                if rng.random() < 0.72:
                    amp = rng.uniform(0.05, 0.45) / (m + 1)
                    y += amp * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * m * x + rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
            out.append(_norm_arr(np.maximum(y, eps)))

        for _ in range(5):
            y = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
            for m in (1, 2, 3):
                if rng.random() < 0.7:
                    amp = rng.uniform(0.05, 0.4) / (m + 0.5)
                    y += amp * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * m * x + rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
            out.append(_norm_arr(np.maximum(y, eps)))

        return out[: max(1, int(count))]

    def _make_starts(base, n, rng, count=20):
        n = int(n)
        if n <= 1:
            return [base.copy()]
        base = _norm_arr(base)
        starts = [base.copy()]

        for sig in (0.01, 0.03, 0.06, 0.10, 0.18, 0.30, 0.45):
            y = base * np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, sig, n))
            starts.append(_norm_arr(np.maximum(y, 1e-12)))

        sym = 0.5 * (base + base[::-1])
        starts.append(_norm_arr(sym))

        for _ in range(8):
            alpha = rng.uniform(0.3, 0.85)
            r = np.maximum(rng.random(n), 1e-6)
            starts.append(_norm_arr(alpha * base + (1.0 - alpha) * r))

        for h in _heuristic_batch(n, rng, 12):
            starts.append(h)

        scored = []
        for s in starts:
            try:
                sc = _internal_score(s)
            except Exception:
                sc = float("inf")
            if np.isfinite(sc):
                scored.append((sc, s))
        scored.sort(key=lambda z: z[0])
        return [s for _, s in scored[: max(1, int(count))]]

    def _resample(arr, nt):
        arr = _norm_arr(arr)
        nt = int(max(1, nt))
        if arr.size == 1:
            return np.full(nt, float(arr[0]), dtype=np.float64)
        x_old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, arr.size, dtype=np.float64)
        x_new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, nt, dtype=np.float64)
        y = np.interp(x_new, x_old, arr)
        return _norm_arr(y)

    def _smooth_polish(a0, stop):
        if minimize is None:
            return a0, _M(a0)
        a = _norm_arr(a0)
        n = int(a.size)
        best_a = a.copy()
        best_M = _M(a)

        for factor in (1.0 / 18.0, 1.0 / 36.0, 1.0 / 72.0, 1.0 / 144.0, 1.0 / 288.0):
            now = time.monotonic()
            rem = stop - now
            if rem < 3.0:
                break
            Mcur = _M(best_a)
            eps = max(1e-10, float(Mcur) * float(factor))
            z0 = np.log(best_a + 1e-12)

            def fun(z):
                if not np.all(np.isfinite(z)):
                    return 1e8, np.zeros_like(z, dtype=np.float64)
                p = _softmax_arr(z)
                A = _build_A(p)
                c = A @ p
                cm = float(c.max())
                if not np.isfinite(cm):
                    return 1e8, np.zeros_like(z, dtype=np.float64)
                s = (c - cm) / eps
                s -= float(s.max())
                w = np.exp(s)
                ws = float(w.sum())
                if ws <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(ws):
                    w = w / (np.linalg.norm(w) + 1e-30)
                    ws = float(w.sum())
                    if ws <= 0.0:
                        return cm, np.zeros_like(z, dtype=np.float64)
                w = w / ws
                L = cm + eps * math.log(ws)
                grad_p = 2.0 * (w @ A)
                grad_z = p * (grad_p - float(np.dot(p, grad_p)))
                if not np.all(np.isfinite(grad_z)):
                    grad_z = np.zeros_like(p, dtype=np.float64)
                return float(L), grad_z

            time_left = stop - time.monotonic()
            maxiter = int(max(25, min(220, max(1, time_left * 2))))
            try:
                res = minimize(
                    fun,
                    z0,
                    jac=True,
                    method="L-BFGS-B",
                    options={"maxiter": maxiter, "ftol": 1e-15, "gtol": 1e-8, "disp": False},
                )
                p = _softmax_arr(res.x if res.x is not None else z0)
            except Exception:
                p = best_a.copy()

            if np.all(np.isfinite(p)):
                p = _norm_arr(p)
                Mp = _M(p)
                if Mp < best_M - 1e-13:
                    best_a = p.copy()
                    best_M = Mp
                cand, Mc = _line_search_mix(best_a, p, best_M, coarse=32, fine=48)
                if cand is not None and Mc < best_M - 1e-13:
                    best_a = cand
                    best_M = Mc

        return best_a, best_M

    # Quick heuristic screening before expensive optimization.
    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 5.0:
        n0 = int(best_seq.size)
        heurs = _heuristic_batch(n0, rng_global, 28)
        for h in heurs:
            _consider(h, force=False)

    # Phase 1: deep best-response optimization from the best current start.
    al1 = min(585.0, 0.45 * budget)
    stop1 = min(deadline - 45.0, t0 + al1)
    if time.monotonic() < stop1 and best_seq.size > 1:
        a1, _ = _optimize(best_seq, stop1, seed=int(seed) + 1, lp_every=1, mint_every=8)
        _consider(a1, force=True)

    # Phase 2: smooth polish.
    al2 = min(150.0, 0.12 * budget)
    stop2 = min(deadline - 35.0, time.monotonic() + al2)
    if time.monotonic() < stop2 and best_seq.size > 1:
        a2, _ = _smooth_polish(best_seq, stop2)
        _consider(a2, force=True)

    # Phase 3: multiple starts in the best sequence length.
    remaining = deadline - 30.0 - time.monotonic()
    if remaining > 45.0 and best_seq.size > 1:
        multi_time = min(0.28 * budget, remaining * 0.62)
        if multi_time > 30.0:
            n0 = int(best_seq.size)
            starts = _make_starts(best_seq, n0, rng_global, count=24)
            n_starts = max(1, min(len(starts), int(multi_time // 22)))
            per = multi_time / float(max(1, n_starts))
            for i, st in enumerate(starts[:n_starts]):
                now = time.monotonic()
                if now > deadline - 28.0:
                    break
                stop = min(deadline - 28.0, now + min(per, 75.0))
                if stop <= now:
                    continue
                try:
                    ac, _ = _optimize(st, stop, seed=int(seed) + 50 + i, lp_every=1, mint_every=10)
                    _consider(ac, force=True)
                except Exception:
                    pass

    # Phase 4: nearby / other lengths, but keep this secondary.
    remaining = deadline - 22.0 - time.monotonic()
    if remaining > 28.0 and best_seq.size > 1:
        len_time = min(0.10 * budget, remaining * 0.72)
        if len_time > 18.0:
            n0 = int(best_seq.size)
            cand_lengths = set()
            for d in (-16, -8, -4, -2, -1, 1, 2, 4, 8, 16, 32, 64, 128):
                nt = n0 + d
                if 64 <= nt <= 512:
                    cand_lengths.add(nt)
            cand_lengths.add(max(64, min(512, n0 // 2)))
            cand_lengths.add(max(65, min(512, n0 + 100)))
            cand_lengths.add(max(65, min(512, n0*2)))
            lengths = sorted(cand_lengths, key=lambda z: (abs(z - n0), z))

            n_len = max(1, min(len(lengths), int(len_time // 18)))
            per = len_time / float(max(1, n_len))
            for idx, nt in enumerate(lengths[:n_len]):
                now = time.monotonic()
                if now > deadline - 18.0:
                    break
                stop = min(deadline - 18.0, now + min(per, 55.0))
                if stop <= now:
                    continue
                try:
                    init = _resample(best_seq, nt)
                    ac, _ = _optimize(init, stop, seed=int(seed) + 1000 + nt, lp_every=2, mint_every=12)
                    _consider(ac, force=True)
                except Exception:
                    pass

    # Phase 5: final polish on the global best.
    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 8.0 and best_seq.size > 1:
        a5, _ = _smooth_polish(best_seq, max(time.monotonic() + 1.0, deadline - 5.0))
        _consider(a5, force=True)

    if time.monotonic() < deadline - 3.0 and best_seq.size > 1:
        a6, _ = _optimize(best_seq, deadline - 2.0, seed=int(seed) + 99999, lp_every=2, mint_every=12)
        _consider(a6, force=True)

    final = [float(x) for x in best_seq]
    if len(final) == 0:
        final = [1.0]

    try:
        vals = np.asarray(final, dtype=np.float64)
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(vals)):
            raise ValueError
        try:
            final_check = float(eval_func(final)) if eval_func is not None else float("nan")
        except Exception:
            final_check = float("inf")
        if (not np.isfinite(final_check)):
            n_fb = max(1, int(best_seq.size) if best_seq.size else 201)
            final = [float(1.0 / n_fb)] * n_fb
    except Exception:
        final = [1.0]

    return final
```
